<p id="mwGg"><b id="mwGw">Hundru Falls</b> is a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Waterfall" title="Waterfall" id="mwHA">waterfall</a> located in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ranchi_district" title="Ranchi district" id="mwHQ">Ranchi District</a> in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/India" title="India" id="mwHg">Indian</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/States_and_territories_of_India" title="States and territories of India" class="mw-redirect" id="mwHw">state</a> of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jharkhand" title="Jharkhand" id="mwIA">Jharkhand</a>. It is the 34th <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_waterfalls_in_India_by_height" title="List of waterfalls in India by height" id="mwIQ">highest waterfall in India</a>,<sup about="#mwt26" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-database_3-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"database"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-database-3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-database-3" id="mwIg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJA">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJQ">]</span></span></a></sup> and one of the most famous tourist places in the region.<sup about="#mwt29" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-4" id="mwJg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwJw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKA">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwKg" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwKw" aria-labelledby="Geography"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Geography">Geography</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Hundru_Falls&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Geography"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwLA">Hundru Falls are located on the course of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Subarnarekha_River" title="Subarnarekha River" id="mwLQ">Subarnarekha River</a> where it falls from a height of <span about="#mwt30" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwLg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"98"},"2":{"wt":"m"}},"i":0}}]}'>98 metres (322</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt30"> </span><span about="#mwt30">ft)</span>, creating one of the highest waterfalls in the state. The spectacular scene of the waterfalls has been described as "a sight to behold".<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt31" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwLw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"citation needed","href":"./Template:Citation_needed"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"December 2024"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_December_2024" about="#mwt31"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt31" id="mwMA"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (December 2024)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup> The different formations of rock due to the erosion by the constantly falling of water have added to the beauty of the place.<sup about="#mwt34" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-maps_5-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"maps"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-maps-5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-maps-5" id="mwMQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwMg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwMw">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt37" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwNQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwNg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNw">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt40" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-admin_7-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"admin"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-admin-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-admin-7" id="mwOQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwOg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOw">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwPQ">Hundru Falls, located at one of the edges of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chota_Nagpur_Plateau#Ranchi_plateau" title="Chota Nagpur Plateau" id="mwPg">Ranchi plateau</a>, is one of the several <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fault_scarp" title="Fault scarp" id="mwPw">scarp</a> falls in the region.<sup about="#mwt43" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-8" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-8" id="mwQA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwQQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQg">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQw">]</span></span></a></sup> During the rainy season it takes a formidable form, but in the dry season it turns into an exciting picnic spot.<sup about="#mwt46" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-9" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-9" id="mwRA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwRQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRg">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRw">]</span></span></a></sup> At the base of the falls is a pool that serves as a bathing place.<sup about="#mwt47" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-maps_5-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"maps"}}'><a href="#cite_note-maps-5" id="mwSA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSg">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt50" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-10" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-10" id="mwTA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwTQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTg">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwUA">Hundru Falls is an example of a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Knickpoint" title="Knickpoint" id="mwUQ">knickpoint</a>, caused by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rejuvenation_(river)" title="Rejuvenation (river)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwUg">rejuvenation</a>. A knickpoint, also called a nick point or simply <i id="mwUw">nick</i>, represents a break in slopes in the longitudinal profile of a river. The break in channel gradient allows water to fall vertically, giving rise to a waterfall.<sup about="#mwt55" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-11" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-11" id="mwVA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwVQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVg">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwWA" aria-labelledby="Transport"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Transport">Transport</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Hundru_Falls&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Transport"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwWQ">To reach Hundru Falls, one has to travel about <span about="#mwt56" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwWg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"21"},"2":{"wt":"km"}},"i":0}}]}'>21 kilometres (13</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt56"> </span><span about="#mwt56">mi)</span> from the main road, Purulia Road.<sup about="#mwt57" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-admin_7-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"admin"}}'><a href="#cite_note-admin-7" id="mwWw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwXA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXQ">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt58" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-12" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-12" id="mwXw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwYA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYQ">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
 
<p id="mwYw">There is also a shortcut and simple four-lane road from Ranchi via <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ormanjhi" title="Ormanjhi" id="mwZA">Ormanjhi</a> via Sikidiri to Hundru. From this road the distance is about <span about="#mwt59" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwZQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"39"},"2":{"wt":"km"}},"i":0}}]}'>39 kilometres (24</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt59"> </span><span about="#mwt59">mi)</span> which is  about <span about="#mwt60" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwZg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"6"},"2":{"wt":"km"}},"i":0}}]}'>6 kilometres (3.7</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt60"> </span><span about="#mwt60">mi)</span> short from the normal road.</p>

<p id="mwZw">There is also a Suvarna Rekha Hydral Project located down the falls, which is a good place for tourists.<sup about="#mwt61" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-admin_7-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"admin"}}'><a href="#cite_note-admin-7" id="mwaA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwaQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwag">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
